# This file contains a list of the test cases written by SWE agents - numbered test0, test1, etc.
# Each new test case should just increment the test number.
#
# Template (YAML):
# tests:
#   - id: testX
#     title: One-line title
#     type: unit|integration|e2e|manual
#     preconditions: e.g., iPhone 12, iOS 15, camera permission granted
#     steps:
#       - Step 1
#       - Step 2
#     expected: Short expected result
#     pass_criteria: Measurable pass criteria
#     automated: true|false
#     automation_path: path/to/test (if automated)
#
# Example:
# tests:
#   - id: test0
#     title: Capture action stores image in buffer
#     type: integration
#     preconditions: App running on iPhone 12 (iOS 15), camera permission granted
#     steps:
#       - step1: Open the camera view
#       - step2: Tap the white capture button
#     expected: An image is captured and stored in the temporary buffer
#     pass_criteria: buffer non-empty and capture recorded within 200ms of tap
#     automated: false
#     automation_path: null
#
#   - id: test1
#     title: Capture button visible bottom-center
#     type: e2e
#     preconditions: App running on iPhone 12 (iOS 15)
#     steps:
#       - step1: Open camera view in portrait
#       - step2: Observe UI layout
#     expected: White capture button is visible bottom-center across supported devices
#     pass_criteria: visible on iPhone12/13/14 portrait
#     automated: false
#     automation_path: null

tests:
  - id: test0
    title: Valid manifest passes validation
    type: unit
    preconditions: validator module available, PyYAML installed
    steps:
      - step1: Create a kinnoo.yaml with all required fields and valid values
      - step2: Call validate(path)
    expected: Returns (True, [])
    pass_criteria: is_valid is True and errors list is empty
    covers:
      - feature: feature1
        ac: AC1
    automated: true
    automation_path: tests/test_validator.py::test_valid_manifest_passes

  - id: test1

        ac: AC2
    automated: true
